AssistantMessage(content=[TextBlock(text='Fix complete and verified — `./gradlew clean assembleDebug` reports **BUILD SUCCESSFUL**, and checkstyle passes.\n\n## Diagnosis\n\n`MainActivity.onBackPressed()` handled only two cases: an open nav drawer, or "am I on HomeFragment?". For any other fragment it unconditionally replaced the content with `HomeFragment`. There was no mechanism at all for a fragment to intercept the back press, so the expanded "View Files" bottom sheet (the orange slider) was ignored and the user was thrown back to Home.\n\n## The patch\n\n**1. New interface** — `interfaces/OnBackPressedInterface.java`:\n```java\npublic interface OnBackPressedInterface {\n    boolean onBackPressed();   // true = fragment consumed the event\n}\n```\n\n**2. Shared helper** — added a static method to `util/BottomSheetUtils.java`, next to the existing `showHideSheet()`, so the collapse logic lives in one place:\n```java\npublic static boolean collapseSheetIfExpanded(BottomSheetBehavior sheetBehavior) {\n    if (sheetBehavior != null && sheetBehavior.getState() == BottomSheetBehavior.STATE_EXPANDED) {\n        sheetBehavior.setState(BottomSheetBehavior.STATE_COLLAPSED);\n        return true;\n    }\n    return false;\n}\n```\n\n**3. `MainActivity.onBackPressed()`** — gives the current fragment first refusal before the drawer-closed path falls through to its existing Home/double-tap-exit logic (lines 271–276 above).\n\n**4. All 7 bottom-sheet fragments** now implement the interface with a three-line override: `InvertPdfFragment`, `RemovePagesFragment`, `MergeFilesFragment`, `AddImagesFragment`, `PdfToImageFragment`, `RemoveDuplicatePagesFragment`, `SplitFilesFragment`.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Sheet expanded + back → sheet collapses, fragment stays. ✅\n- Sheet collapsed + back → Home, as before.\n- Drawer open → still closes first (checked before the fragment).\n- HomeFragment → double-tap-to-exit unchanged.\n\nTwo notes on design choices: I gated on `STATE_EXPANDED` only (rather than "anything not collapsed") so a sheet mid-`STATE_SETTLING` toward collapsed doesn\'t silently swallow a back press.
